  - **Fix:** Set the name field imeAction=Next with keyboardActions that, when the row is the last and non-blank, append a new ShoppingItemDraft.
- ⚪ **[missing-feature] Metadata footer missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:75,488-516` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS shows a centered footer below the launcher (sparkles + deals.footerLive/footerEstimate depending on dataSource, plus deals.footerSchedule explaining the 6h refresh). Android has no footer.
  - **Fix:** Add a centered metadataFooter Composable rendered when promotions are loaded, using dataSource==live_web_search to pick footerLive vs footerEstimate plus footerSchedule. Add deals.footerLive/footerEstimate/footerSchedule L10n keys (absent).
- ⚪ **[visual] NBEmptyState double-wrapped in NBCard**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:649-654,997-1006` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:240-245,690-695`
  - Android wraps NBEmptyState inside NBCard for the trending-empty and analyze-empty states, but NBEmptyState already applies nbCard (border+shadow+radius.lg). This produces a card-inside-a-card with doubled borders/padding, unlike iOS where NBEmptyState is rendered bare.
  - **Fix:** Remove the surrounding NBCard{ } wrappers around NBEmptyState calls (lines 240-245 and 690-695); render NBEmptyState directly.
- ⚪ **[visual] NBEmptyState rendered without icon**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:650,998,1012` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:241-244,691-694`
  - iOS always passes a systemImage to NBEmptyState (doc.text for analyze, tag.slash/line.3.decrease for promos). Android calls NBEmptyState with no icon argument, so the empty states render without the iconic badge that iOS shows.
  - **Fix:** Pass an appropriate ImageVector icon to each NBEmptyState (e.g. Icons.Outlined.Description for receipts, a tag/filter icon for promos) to match iOS.
- ⚪ **[visual] Shopping result card shadow/radius mismatch**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:832,1569` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:433,768`
  - iOS shoppingResultCard and receiptAnalyzeCard use .nbCard(radius: Theme.Radius.md, shadow: Theme.Shadow.sm). Android renders both with NBCard(radius = Radius.lg, shadow = Shadow.lg) — larger corner radius and heavier shadow than iOS.
  - **Fix:** Change ShoppingResultCard and ReceiptAnalyzeCard NBCard calls to radius = SolvioTheme.Radius.md, shadow = SolvioTheme.Shadow.sm to match iOS.
- ⚪ **[visual] Best-store result omits checkmark badge and savings-label styling**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:1383-1426,1698-1747` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:433-457,598-620`
  - iOS shoppingResultCard shows an NBIconBadge checkmark.seal.fill (success) on the right of the hero row and a right-aligned savings column with shoppingList.savingsLabel. Android puts a DataSourceBadge on the right instead (no checkmark seal) and renders the savings column left-aligned next to total. iOS also adds a FreshnessFooter via freshnessFooter; Android's FreshnessFooter exists but uses different keys (shoppingList.freshAt/cacheLive/cacheStale/cacheRefresh vs iOS asOfFmt/cacheFresh/cacheStale/cacheLive).
  - **Fix:** Add the checkmark.seal.fill success icon badge to the hero row, keep DataSourceBadge where iOS has it, and align the freshness footer keys/labels with iOS (asOfFmt + cacheStateLabel fresh/stale/live).
- ⚪ **[copy-l10n] Shopping row placeholder uses different L10n keys than iOS**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:1336,1355` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:407,411-415`
  - iOS shopping rows use shoppingList.itemPlaceholder ('np. mleko 2%') and shoppingList.qtyPlaceholder. Android uses shoppingList.itemNamePh ('np. mleko') for the name and no placeholder for qty. Two parallel key sets exist (itemNamePh/qty vs itemPlaceholder/qtyPlaceholder), causing drift in placeholder copy.
  - **Fix:** Switch the Android NBTextField placeholders to shoppingList.itemPlaceholder and shoppingList.qtyPlaceholder (which already exist in Android L10n) to match iOS, and retire the duplicate itemNamePh/qty keys.
- ⚪ **[behavior] navTitle / inline navigation bar not applied on Android**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:82-83` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS sets .navigationTitle(deals.navTitle) with inline display mode for the Okazje screen. The Android OkazjeHubScreen has no top app bar / title wiring inside the composable (it relies on whatever parent scaffold provides), and deals.navTitle is defined but not referenced here.
  - **Fix:** Ensure the hosting Scaffold/TopAppBar shows deals.navTitle for this destination to match iOS's inline nav title.
- ⚪ **[visual] Analyze section shows section-level subtitle that iOS moved into the disclosure row**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:538-562,609-614` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:335-339,679-683`
  - Because Android did not adopt the disclosure-card pattern, both AI sections render a standalone NBSectionHeader + a separate caption subtitle line (analyze.subtitle / shoppingList.subtitle) above the body. iOS moved these subtitles into the collapsed disclosure trigger row, so the standalone subtitle line is extra/visually inconsistent.
  - **Fix:** When porting the disclosure-card pattern, move analyze.subtitle and shoppingList.subtitle into the disclosure header row (as iOS does) instead of a separate caption under the section header.
- ⚪ **[visual] Remove-row affordance differs (✕ text vs minus.circle, no count-disable parity)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:1368-1376` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:417-423`
  - iOS uses a minus.circle SF symbol (16, muted) that is disabled when items.count<=1 but still rendered. Android uses a '✕' text glyph that is conditionally NOT rendered when size<=1 (onRemove null). Minor visual + behavioral difference (icon vs glyph; hidden vs disabled).
  - **Fix:** Use a minus-circle vector icon shown-but-disabled when only one row remains, to match iOS.
- ⚪ **[behavior] No per-screen receipts/promotions caching; reloads every entry**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Deals/OkazjeHubView.swift:84-89,109-123` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/deals/OkazjeHubScreen.kt:113-133,173-176`
  - iOS reads from a shared AppDataStore (store.promotions/receipts already prefetched on login, store.ensurePromotions/ensureReceipts only fetch if absent). Android's OkazjeHubViewModel always calls PromotionsRepo.fetch() and ReceiptsRepo.list() fresh in LaunchedEffect(Unit) with no shared cache, so every navigation to the tab triggers a full (slow, LLM-backed) refetch and there is no cross-screen reuse of the already-known totalPotentialSavings.
  - **Fix:** Back the screen with a shared store/cache (ensure-style fetch that reuses prefetched data) instead of unconditional fetch on every entry, mirroring AppDataStore.ensurePromotions/ensureReceipts.

## Dashboard — parity ~12% (27 findings)

_The Android Dashboard (DashboardScreen.kt, ~155 LOC) is a thin stub that renders only three things: a screen header (no refresh button), a single hero card (eyebrow + "rolling 30" + total amount + ONE txn-count tag + two non-functional buttons), and a flat list of the first 10 raw expenses under a generic "Expenses" title. The iOS Dashboard (DashboardView.swift ~1162 LOC + DailyStackedChartCard.swift ~436 LOC) is a rich, fully-aggregated dashboard with ~14 distinct sections: hero with avg/day + MoM tags + total-budget progress bar, no-recent-data / empty states, recent-activity rows (icon+category+date subtitle, tappable to detail), over-budget alerts, anomaly alerts, forecast+savings-rate row, 4 metric stat tiles, an interactive stacked daily chart (range picker, legend toggle, tap-a-day breakdown sheet), top-categories bar chart with legend, spending-mix stacked bar, financial wellness score with sub-scores, per-category budget progress rows, and an AI-insights CTA. Crucially, Android does NONE of the client-side aggregation that iOS performs in DashboardDisplay.build (MoM, forecast, weekly spend, savings rate, wellness, anomalies, over-budget, per-category spend, currency conversion via exchangeRate). Android also has NO pull-to-refresh, NO haptics, NO skeleton loading (uses a single spinner card instead of NBSkeletonHero+NBSkeletonList which exist), NO currency conversion of foreign-currency expenses, NO recent-expense sorting, NO category icon/subtitle on rows, and the two hero buttons (Scan/Add) are wired to empty {} lambdas. The shared atoms needed (NBStatTile, NBProgressBar, NBSectionHeader with trailing, NBEmptyState, NBSkeletonHero/List, NBIconBadge, NBRow) and almost all L10n keys already exist on Android, so the gap is purely unimplemented screen code. This is roughly a 90% feature gap._

- 🔴 **[missing-feature] No client-side aggregation: Android shows raw total instead of DashboardDisplay computations**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:885-1160` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:96-101`
  - iOS builds a DashboardDisplay off-main (DashboardDisplay.build) computing totalSpent (currency-converted), avgDaily over actual date span, mostExpensive, thisWeekSpent, categorySpending top-5 with stable color indices, dailySpending, dailySlices (90d), budgetRows, totalBudget/remaining/progress, momChange, monthlyForecast, savingsRate, overBudget, anomalies, wellness sub-scores, recent (sorted). Android computes only `total = expenses.sumOf { it.amount.toDouble() }` with no conversion and no other aggregates.
  - **Fix:** Port DashboardDisplay.build into a Kotlin computeDisplay(DashboardResponse) -> DashboardDisplay data class (run off the main thread in the ViewModel, e.g. withContext(Dispatchers.Default)). Reproduce every aggregate: currency conversion via expense.exchangeRate, avgDaily over earliest-date span, top-5 categorySpending with categoryColorIndex, budget rows/total/remaining/progress, momChange from prevTotal/prevByCategory/prevExpenses, monthlyForecast via month progress, thisWeek (Mon->today), overBudget (>=80%, top 3), anomalies (curr>15 && prev>0 && curr/prev>=1.5, top 2), savingsRate, and wellness sub-scores.
- 🔴 **[bug] Foreign-currency expenses not converted (wrong totals)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:890-898` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:100,150`
  - iOS converts each expense: if expense.currency != display currency and exchangeRate present, raw*rate. Android sums expenses.sumOf { it.amount.toDouble() } with no conversion, so multi-currency users see incorrect totals; the per-row ExpenseRow uses e.currency ?: currency only for display formatting, not conversion.
  - **Fix:** Apply the iOS convert() logic before summing and before per-row display: convert(e) = if (e.currency?.uppercase() != currency && e.exchangeRate != null) raw*rate else raw. Display converted amounts in rows too.
- 🟠 **[bug] Hero buttons (Scan / Add expense) are non-functional placeholders**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:228-276,286-295` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:113-116`
  - Android hero renders NBPrimaryButton(label=dashboard.scan, onClick={}) and NBSecondaryButton(label=dashboard.addExpense, onClick={}) with empty lambdas. iOS hero has NO such buttons at all; instead the empty/no-recent-data card has the Scan/Go-to-expenses actions and they navigate (router.showingScanSheet / router.selectedTab). Android both invents buttons in the wrong place AND leaves them dead.
  - **Fix:** Remove the two hero buttons. Move Scan/Add actions into the no-recent-data and empty-state cards as iOS does, and wire them to real navigation (open scan sheet, switch to expenses tab).
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Missing refresh button in header + no pull-to-refresh**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:90-117,57-65` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:86-90,80-84`
  - iOS header has a trailing 40x40 muted refresh button (arrow.clockwise, bordered, with haptic) and the ScrollView is .refreshable (pull-to-refresh with light/success haptics). Android NBScreenHeader is called without a trailing param and LazyColumn has no refresh affordance at all.
  - **Fix:** Pass trailing={ refresh IconButton } to NBScreenHeader (40dp muted box, 2px border, radius sm, arrow.clockwise icon). Wrap the list in a PullRefresh / pullToRefresh modifier that calls vm.load(), and add haptics.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Hero missing avgDaily tag, MoM-change tag, and total-budget progress section**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:121-195` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:102-118`
  - iOS hero shows: a credit-card NBIconBadge (size 36) top-right, then THREE NBTag chips (txns, avgDaily+/day, and a colored momChange '+N% vs prev' green/red), plus a full budgetProgressSection (uppercase 'BUDGET PROGRESS' label, remaining/over amount colored, NBProgressBar, 'X% of <budget> used') when totalBudget>0. Android hero shows only the single txns tag and no icon badge, no avg/day, no MoM, no budget progress.
  - **Fix:** Add NBIconBadge(creditcard, 36) to the hero HStack. Render the avgDaily tag and conditional momChange tag (green bg/fg when negative, destructive when positive). Add budgetProgressSection composable bordered with radius md when totalBudget>0.
- 🟠 **[visual] Recent-expenses section: wrong title, no section header trailing 'See all', no icon/subtitle, no cap/sort**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:621-672,1112-1128` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:120-129,135-154`
  - iOS recentExpenses uses NBSectionHeader(eyebrow='LATEST', title='Recent activity', trailing='SEE ALL' button -> expenses tab), shows up to 8 expenses sorted by date desc as NBRow with NBIconBadge (doc.text.fill for receipt-linked, creditcard.fill otherwise), title, and a subtitle '<dayMonth> · <category>', amount in mono. Android shows a plain Text(locale.t('expenses.title')) header (wrong key/copy), then take(10) unsorted ExpenseRow with no icon, subtitle = Fmt.date(e.date) only (no category), and no See-all action.
  - **Fix:** Replace with NBSectionHeader(eyebrow=dashboard.latestEyebrow, title=dashboard.recentActivity, trailing= See-all -> expenses tab). Sort by date desc, take(8). Each row: NBIconBadge by receiptId, title, subtitle 'dayMonth · categoryName', mono amount. Use Fmt.dayMonth (add it) not Fmt.date.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Missing entire 'No recent data' card and onboarding empty state**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:202-322` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS noRecentDataCard: when 30d window empty but history exists, shows an info-tinted card (calendar.badge.clock badge, title/subtitle, archive stat grid of totalReceipts/totalExpenses, and Go-to-expenses + Scan-receipt buttons). When no history at all, shows NBEmptyState(wallet.pass, emptyTitle, emptySubtitle, Scan action). Android has neither; it just shows the (always-present) hero + list, so a brand-new user sees an empty hero with $0 and no guidance.
  - **Fix:** Add noRecentDataCard composable: info card with archive stats grid + two action buttons when totalTransactions==0 && hasHistory; NBEmptyState when totalTransactions==0 && !hasHistory.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Missing over-budget alerts section**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:366-398,1051-1064` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS overBudgetAlerts renders up to 3 categories at >=80% spent: warning/destructive triangle icon, category name, '<pct>% used/exceeded', 'spent / budget' mono, tinted bg+border (warning if <100%, destructive if >=100%). Android has nothing.
  - **Fix:** Compute overBudget list (>=0.8, sorted desc, take 3) and render the alert rows with warning/destructive coloring exactly as iOS.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Missing 4 metric stat tiles (Receipts / Biggest / This week / Top category)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:432-439` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS metricCards renders a 2x2 LazyVGrid of NBStatTile: Receipts (count, 'AI processed'), Biggest (mostExpensive amount, 'largest txn'), This week (thisWeekSpent, 'weekly spend'), Top category (name, 'highest spend'). NBStatTile already exists on Android (NBComponents.kt:343) but is never used here.
  - **Fix:** Add a 2-col grid of four NBStatTile cards using receiptsCount, mostExpensive, thisWeekSpent, topCategory with the matching sub-labels.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Missing interactive daily stacked-by-category chart card**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DailyStackedChartCard.swift:14-292` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS DailyStackedChartCard (whole file) renders: section header, a 7D/14D/30D range segmented picker, a stacked-by-category bar chart (height 200) with x-axis day labels and y grid, a horizontally-scrolling legend where tapping toggles category visibility (strikethrough when hidden), a summary line 'Total (Nd): <amount>', and tap-a-bar -> DayBreakdownSheet. Android has none of this and no charting at all.
  - **Fix:** Build a DailyStackedChartCard composable using a Compose charting approach (e.g. Vico or custom Canvas) with the range picker, toggleable legend (hiddenCategoryIds set), summary line, and a tap handler that opens a day-breakdown bottom sheet. Source data from dailySlices/allExpensesConverted.
- 🟠 **[missing-popup] Missing day-breakdown bottom sheet (tap a chart bar)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DailyStackedChartCard.swift:301-435` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS DayBreakdownSheet: presented when a chart bar is tapped; NavigationStack with date title, hero total, '<n> expenses' plural, per-category list (color swatch + name + amount), per-expense list (NBIconBadge, title, category swatch+name, amount, tappable -> expenseDetail), empty state, medium/large detents, drag indicator, Close action. Android has no chart and therefore no breakdown sheet.
  - **Fix:** Add a ModalBottomSheet DayBreakdownSheet with the same sections (categories + expenses), localized title via Fmt.date, plural txn count, and tap-through to expense detail.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Missing top-categories bar chart + legend**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:501-547,608-617` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS categoryChart renders (when categorySpending non-empty) a horizontal BarMark chart of top-5 categories colored by stable colorIndex (chart1..6 palette), height = max(160, count*36), plus a legend list (swatch + name + amount). Android absent.
  - **Fix:** Add a top-categories horizontal bar chart card with the same 6-color palette mapping (categoryColor index%6 -> chart1..chart6) and a legend list.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing anomaly alerts section**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:402-428,1072-1082` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS anomalies renders up to 2 categories where curr>15 && prev>0 && curr/prev>=1.5: emoji icon, category name, 'X.Xx more times' in info color, info-tinted card. Android absent.
  - **Fix:** Compute anomalies from spentByCat vs prevSpentByCat (top 2 by ratio) and render info-tinted rows with the category emoji icon and 'N.Nx more' text.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing forecast + savings-rate stat row**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:326-362,966-972,1066-1069` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS forecastAndSavings shows a 2-col grid (when totalSpent>0 and forecast or rate exists): smallStat gauge 'Monthly forecast' = amount, and smallStat banknote 'Savings rate' = N% colored (green>=20, warning>=10, else destructive). Android absent.
  - **Fix:** Add a 2-col Row of smallStat cards for monthlyForecast and savingsRate with iOS color thresholds.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing spending-mix stacked proportion bar**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:560-606` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS categoryPieChart/stackedMixBar renders a single 32pt-high horizontal stacked bar where each category's width is proportional to its share, bordered, plus a legend list with % per category. Android absent.
  - **Fix:** Add a spending-mix card: a Row of colored weight-proportional segments (height 32dp, radius 4, border) and a legend with percentage per category.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing financial wellness score section**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:443-497,1084-1110` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS wellness renders (when wellnessScore != nil) a section: big 'NN' /100 in an 80x80 muted bordered box, plus scoreRow bars for Savings (/40), Budget (/40), Trend (/20) using NBProgressBar. Android absent (no aggregation and no UI).
  - **Fix:** Compute savingsSub/budgetSub/trendSub/wellness exactly as iOS and render the score box + three NBProgressBar score rows.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing per-category budget overview section**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:676-701,937-943` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS budgetOverview renders (when budgetRows non-empty) NBSectionHeader 'CATEGORY PROGRESS' and a card per category with name, 'spent / budget' mono, and NBProgressBar(over = pct>1). Android absent.
  - **Fix:** Build budgetRows (categories with budget>0, sorted by spent desc) and render a card-per-category with NBProgressBar (NBProgressBar exists at NBComponents.kt:783).
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing AI Insights CTA card at bottom of dashboard**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:705-750` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS aiInsightsCTA renders a tappable lg card: eyebrow + 'AI insights' title + sparkles NBIconBadge (info, 40), subtitle, and an action chip ('<action>' + arrow.right) that navigates to More > Analysis. Android absent.
  - **Fix:** Add an AI-insights CTA NBCard (radius lg, shadow lg) with sparkles badge and action chip navigating to the Analysis screen.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Loading state uses single spinner card instead of skeleton hero+list**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:24-30` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:94`
  - iOS cold-load shows NBSkeletonHero() + NBSkeletonList(rows:3) so the placeholder matches the eventual layout. Android shows a single NBLoadingCard() (spinner). NBSkeletonHero and NBSkeletonList already exist on Android (NBComponents.kt:678,672) but are unused.
  - **Fix:** Replace NBLoadingCard() in the Loading branch with NBSkeletonHero() followed by NBSkeletonList(rows = 3).
- 🟡 **[behavior] Recent-expense rows not tappable to expense detail in correct way; whole-list cap differs**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:644-648` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:127-129,135-154`
  - iOS uses NBRow with prefix(8) and pushes router.push(.expenseDetail(id:)). Android ExpenseRow uses onClick=onOpenExpense (good) but is a plain Row styled card (not NBRow with press animation), takes 10 not 8, and is unsorted. The press feedback/animation and ordering differ.
  - **Fix:** Use NBRow (exists, NBComponents.kt:404) for the 2px press animation, sort expenses by date desc, and take(8) to match iOS.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Missing Fmt helpers (dayMonth, dayMonthShort, parseISO) needed for parity**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:1120,906; DailyStackedChartCard.swift:148` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/core/Formatters.kt:15-30`
  - iOS uses Fmt.dayMonth (recent subtitle), Fmt.dayMonthShort (chart x-axis), Fmt.parseISO (avgDaily span), Fmt.date(dateString) (sheet title). Android Formatters.kt (object Fmt) only has amount() and date(); the others don't exist, so the ported sections cannot format dates the same way.
  - **Fix:** Add Fmt.dayMonth, Fmt.dayMonthShort, and Fmt.parseISO to android core/Formatters.kt matching iOS output formats.
- ⚪ **[visual] Hero total uses no numeric content transition / minimumScaleFactor handling**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:134-142` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:107`
  - iOS total uses AppFont.hero with minimumScaleFactor(0.6), lineLimit(1), contentTransition(.numericText()) and a spring animation on value change. Android renders SolvioFonts.hero plain with no shrink-to-fit or animation, so long amounts may overflow/wrap.
  - **Fix:** Apply maxLines=1, autoSize/softWrap=false with a downscale, and an animated value change (animate*AsState) on the hero amount Text.
- ⚪ **[bug] ViewModel re-wraps DashboardResponse pointlessly and is a no-op mapping**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:949-1069` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:53-60`
  - DashboardViewModel.load() reconstructs a DashboardResponse copying only 5 of 11 fields (drops prevExpenses, monthIncome, savingsTarget, prevTotal, prevByCategory) into `mapped`, which would silently strip exactly the fields iOS needs for MoM/anomalies/savings/wellness. This both is a code smell and actively discards needed data.
  - **Fix:** Store the raw `resp` directly (UiState.Loaded(resp)); do not re-wrap. All fields (incl. prevTotal/prevByCategory/monthIncome) must be retained for the aggregation port.
- ⚪ **[behavior] Currency not upper-cased like iOS**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:886` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:99`
  - iOS uses (settings?.currency ?? 'PLN').uppercased() everywhere. Android uses data.settings?.currency ?: "PLN" with no uppercasing, so a lowercase 'pln' from the API would format differently.
  - **Fix:** currency = (data.settings?.currency ?: "PLN").uppercase().
- ⚪ **[behavior] No haptics anywhere on Android dashboard**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:61,64,98,230,254,630,646,707` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:114-115,128`
  - iOS fires haptics on refresh (impact light), pull-to-refresh threshold + success, see-all/row taps (selection), range/legend taps (selection), scan (impact medium), chart bar tap (impact light). Android has no haptic feedback on any interaction.
  - **Fix:** Add HapticFeedback (LocalHapticFeedback / a Haptics helper) to refresh, row tap, see-all, range picker, legend toggle, scan, and chart-bar tap to match iOS.
- ⚪ **[visual] Total-spent eyebrow not uppercased and missing 'rolling 30' styling parity**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:125-129` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:104-105`
  - iOS hero uses NBEyebrow(text: t('dashboard.totalSpent').uppercased()) then a caption 'rolling 30'. Android passes locale.t('dashboard.totalSpent') to NBEyebrow without .uppercase(); NBEyebrow may already uppercase via styling but iOS explicitly uppercases the source string. Verify casing matches.
  - **Fix:** Pass the eyebrow text uppercased (or confirm NBEyebrow applies textCase uppercase) to match iOS exactly.
- ⚪ **[visual] Section content-spacing/animation parity**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Dashboard/DashboardView.swift:35-54` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/dashboard/DashboardScreen.kt:80-132`
  - iOS VStack uses Theme.Spacing.md between sections, a trailing Spacer(minLength: xl), and a spring animation keyed on totalTransactions. Android LazyColumn uses spacedBy(Spacing.md) (matches) but lacks the bottom xl spacer and the cross-section spring animation; once sections are added, verify ordering matches iOS exactly (hero, noRecentData, recent, overBudget, anomalies, forecast, metrics, dailyTrend, pie, categoryChart, wellness, budgetOverview, aiCTA).
  - **Fix:** Add a final Spacer(height = Spacing.xl) item and replicate the iOS section ordering precisely when implementing the missing sections.

## Expenses — parity ~22% (26 findings)

_The Android Expenses port is a drastically reduced shell compared to iOS. The list screen is essentially a read-only, search-only list: it is missing the entire filter panel (date range, category, amount range, vendor), the entire sort panel (4 fields + direction toggle), batch multi-select + bulk delete, swipe-to-delete, the per-row context menu, vendor logos, the empty/error/loading parity (uses one loading card instead of skeleton rows, no NBEmptyState), the Scan Receipt / Add Expense CTAs, and the whole ExpenseCreate sheet. The detail screen is missing edit (sheet + toolbar pencil + edit button), delete (button + confirm alert), the meta card (date/category/vendor/currency rows), notes card, tags card, the virtual-receipt QR tile and the currency converter tile, and only offers a "Close" button instead of Edit/Delete actions. It also reloads the full expenses list per detail open and per list open via its own ExpensesRepo.list() instead of a shared warm store, so there is no optimistic delete/undo flow at all. On top of the missing features there are concrete bugs: receipt-item quantity prints as a Double ("× 2.0"), prices render "0,00" instead of being hidden when absent, item quantity sits below the name instead of as a leading "Nx" prefix, and list rows use Fmt.date (full date) instead of iOS Fmt.dayMonth. Hardcoded English fallback strings ("Failed", "Not found") bypass the existing L10n keys. Overall this area is far from parity._

- 🔴 **[missing-feature] Entire filters panel missing (date range, category, amount range, vendor)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:337-398` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:104-106`
  - iOS has a collapsible Filters panel with a date-range chip row (All time / This month / Last 30 / This year), a category chip row, an amount From-To range with inverted-range validation and a destructive border + 'validation.amountRange' message, a vendor text filter, and a 'Clear filters' button shown when any filter is active, plus an accent indicator dot on the Filters toolbar button. Android renders a Filters toolbar button that toggles activePanel to Panel.Filters but never draws any panel content — tapping it does nothing visible, and none of the filtering logic exists.
  - **Fix:** Build a filtersPanel composable that appears when activePanel == Filters: horizontal date-range chip Row using chip styling (mono 11 uppercase, foreground bg when active), category chip Row from settings categories, an amount From/To Row with decimal keyboard and inverted-range red border + validation.amountRange caption, a vendor NBTextField, and a Clear-filters button. Wire all of them into the visible() filter. Add the accent indicator dot on the Filters ToolbarBtn when hasActiveFilters. All L10n keys (expenses.allTime/thisMonth/last30/thisYear/allCategories/vendor/vendorPlaceholder/amountFrom/amountTo/clearFilters, validation.amountRange) already exist in core/L10n.kt.
- 🔴 **[missing-feature] Entire sort panel missing (4 sort fields + direction toggle)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:400-451` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:107-112`
  - iOS has a Sort panel with rows for Date / Amount / Title / Vendor; tapping a row toggles direction (asc/desc) with a chevron + 'asc/desc' label, defaults Date->desc and others->asc, and applies a stable secondary sort by createdAt for equal dates. Android renders the Sort toolbar button and toggles activePanel to Panel.Sort but draws no panel and never sorts the list at all — the list is shown in raw API order.
  - **Fix:** Add SortField {date,amount,title,vendor} and SortDir {asc,desc} state to ExpensesViewModel, build a sortPanel composable with the 4 NBDivider-separated rows mirroring iOS (active row tinted with accent.opacity(0.3), arrow + asc/desc label), implement toggleSort, and apply the sort in the visible() computation including the date->createdAt tiebreaker. L10n keys expenses.sortDate/sortAmount/sortTitle/sortVendor/sortAsc/sortDesc exist.
- 🔴 **[missing-popup] Add Expense create sheet entirely missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:95-110,929-1069` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS presents ExpenseCreateSheet (title, amount, date picker, category chips with a fallback CategoryPickerVM load, vendor, tags, notes TextEditor) with Cancel/Save toolbar, validation disabling Save until title non-empty and amount parses, then calls ExpensesRepo.create + toast.created. Android has no create entry point and no create sheet at all; ExpensesRepo.create exists in Repositories.kt but is never called from the UI.
  - **Fix:** Add an ExpenseCreateSheet (ModalBottomSheet or full-screen dialog) mirroring iOS fields and validation, plus a 'New expense' CTA/empty-state action that opens it, wire to ExpensesRepo.create and a success toast, then reload the list. Keys expenses.newExpense/title.label/titlePlaceholder/tags/category, common.amount/date/notes/none/save/cancel exist.
- 🔴 **[missing-feature] Expense edit (sheet + toolbar pencil + edit action button) entirely missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:98-127,397-417,430-573` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:107`
  - iOS detail has a top-bar pencil button and an Edit action button that present ExpenseEditSheet (title, amount, date, category chips, vendor, tags, notes), calling ExpensesRepo.update + toast.updated. Android detail offers only a single 'Close' NBSecondaryButton and no way to edit; ExpensesRepo.update exists but is never invoked.
  - **Fix:** Add an edit affordance (top-bar action or button) opening an ExpenseEditSheet prefilled from the expense, wired to ExpensesRepo.update with success/error toasts, then refresh.
- 🔴 **[missing-feature] Expense delete (button + confirm alert + optimistic pop) missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:128-156,397-417` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS detail has a destructive Delete button that opens a confirm alert, then pops, optimistically removes, calls ExpensesRepo.delete, and toasts deleted/rollback. Android detail has no delete affordance at all.
  - **Fix:** Add a destructive Delete button + confirm dialog (expenseDetail.deleteConfirm / expenses.deleteConfirmMsg) calling ExpensesRepo.delete([id]) then onBack + success toast (expenseDetail.deleted).
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Batch multi-select + bulk delete bar missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:157-213,669-757` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS supports long-press to enter selection mode, a checkbox per row, a selection-count bulk bar with Deselect-all and 'Delete selected (N)' (destructive), and a bulk-delete confirm alert. Android rows are plain clickable navigation only; no selection state, no bulk bar, no bulk delete.
  - **Fix:** Add selectedIds state to the ViewModel, render a checkbox/selection state on each row, long-press to toggle selection, show a bulk bar (count + deselect-all + delete-selected destructive button) above the list, and a bulk-delete confirm dialog calling ExpensesRepo.delete(ids). Keys expenses.selectionMode/deselectAll/deleteSelected/confirmBulkDelete/confirmBulkDeleteDesc exist.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Swipe-to-delete + single-row delete confirm + undo toast missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:641-657,111-130,731-746,781-817` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS rows have trailing full-swipe delete (optimistic remove + 5s Undo toast via scheduleDeleteCommit), a context-menu Delete that opens a confirm alert, and an undoable toast with restore-on-undo and rollback-on-failure. Android list rows have no swipe action, no context menu, no delete path, and no undo toast at all.
  - **Fix:** Implement SwipeToDismiss on each row (or a context/long-press menu) with optimistic removal and a 5s undoable Snackbar mirroring scheduleDeleteCommit (cancelable commit, restore on undo, rollback + error toast on failure). Keys common.delete/itemDeleted/itemsDeleted/undo, expenses.confirmDelete/deleteConfirmMsg exist.
- 🟠 **[behavior] Loading uses single card instead of skeleton rows; no empty-state, no error-vs-empty distinction**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:596-628` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:116-118`
  - iOS shows NBSkeletonList(rows:6) while loading, an NBErrorCard with retry when errored with empty data, and an NBEmptyState (tray icon, 'No expenses' title, 'Add first' subtitle, 'New expense' action — or 'clear filters' messaging when filtered) when the filtered list is empty. Android shows a single NBLoadingCard() while loading and renders nothing when the loaded list is empty (no empty state) and no New-expense CTA.
  - **Fix:** Use NBSkeletonList(rows = 6) for loading (already available in NBComponents.kt:673), and add an NBEmptyState branch (NBComponents.kt:561) for the empty visible list with the 'New expense' action and search/filter-aware copy (expenses.noExpensesUpper/addFirst/newExpense vs common.empty/expenses.clearFilters).
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Scan Receipt / Add Expense top CTAs missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:3-5,622` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS exposes Scan Receipt / Add Expense entry points (doc comment describes top CTAs; empty state also offers 'New expense'). Android list has no add/scan affordance whatsoever — there is no way to create an expense from this screen.
  - **Fix:** Add a header trailing action (NBScreenHeader supports a trailing slot) and/or empty-state action to open the create sheet (and scan flow) so users can add expenses, matching iOS.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Detail meta card (date / category / vendor / currency rows) missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:225-246` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS shows a meta NBCard with label/value rows for Date, Category, Vendor and Currency. Android detail shows only the hero card and (conditionally) receipt items — none of the date/category/vendor/currency meta rows.
  - **Fix:** Add a meta NBCard after the hero with label-left/value-right rows for date, category, vendor and currency, using expenseDetail.date/category/vendor and settings.currency keys.
- 🟠 **[bug] Receipt item quantity renders as Double ('× 2.0') and is positioned below the name instead of leading 'Nx' prefix**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:318-341` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:88-101`
  - iOS renders quantity as a leading column 'Nx' with integer formatting when whole (Int(qty)) else %g, e.g. '2×' or '1.5×', placed before the item name. Android renders it as a trailing-of-column secondary line '× $qty' where qty is a raw Double, producing '× 2.0' and a different layout (below the name, not a leading prefix).
  - **Fix:** Format quantity as integer when whole (else trimmed decimal) and render it as a leading 'Nx' prefix column (minWidth ~30, leading aligned) before the item name, matching iOS itemRow.
- 🟡 **[missing-popup] Per-row context menu missing (Select / Delete)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:731-746` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS long-press shows a contextMenu with 'Select/Deselect' and destructive 'Delete'. Android has no context menu and only a plain clickable row.
  - **Fix:** Add a long-press DropdownMenu on each row with Select/Deselect and Delete entries (Delete opens the confirm dialog).
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Vendor chain logo missing on list rows**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:694,819-823` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:124-142`
  - iOS rows show a VendorLogo (known-retailer logo like Lidl/Biedronka/Kaufland, else a category-icon SF Symbol fallback). Android rows show no leading icon/logo at all — just the title/subtitle column and amount.
  - **Fix:** Add a leading VendorLogo equivalent (resolve known-chain logo by vendor, fall back to category icon or doc.text/creditcard) in the row, matching iOS's iconName(for:) fallback logic.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Search only matches title+vendor; iOS matches title, vendor, notes, categoryName, tags (and is debounced)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:836-861,223-234` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:120-123`
  - iOS search filters across title, vendor, notes, categoryName and tags, and is debounced 250ms to avoid freezes at large datasets. Android only filters title and vendor, with no debounce.
  - **Fix:** Extend the Android filter to also check notes, categoryName and tags (case-insensitive contains), and add a 250ms debounce on the search query before filtering.
- 🟡 **[visual] List subtitle uses full date (Fmt.date) instead of day-month (Fmt.dayMonth)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:708,673` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:135`
  - iOS list rows show a compact day-month date via Fmt.dayMonth(e.date). Android uses Fmt.date(e.date) (full date) because Fmt.dayMonth does not exist in core/Formatters.kt. Also iOS row order is category · date (category first), Android builds listOfNotNull(date, vendor, categoryName) so it shows date · vendor · category — different ordering and an extra vendor token.
  - **Fix:** Add Fmt.dayMonth to core/Formatters.kt and use it in the row; reorder the subtitle to categoryName · dayMonth (drop vendor from the subtitle) to match iOS row layout.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Detail notes card missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:61,248-259` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS renders a notes NBCard (eyebrow 'NOTES' + body text) when notes are non-empty. Android never displays notes.
  - **Fix:** Add a notes NBCard shown when e.notes is non-empty, using common.notes for the eyebrow.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Detail tags card missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:62,261-273` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS renders a tags NBCard (eyebrow 'TAGS' + horizontal NBTag chips) when tags exist. Android never displays tags.
  - **Fix:** Add a tags NBCard shown when e.tags is non-empty, rendering each tag as an NBTag in a horizontal scroll, using expenses.tags for the eyebrow.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Virtual-receipt QR tile missing on detail**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:70-72,343-370` → Android: `absent`
  - When the expense has a receiptId, iOS shows a VirtualReceiptTile (QR + open-in-browser + copy-link for the public /receipt/:id URL). Android shows nothing of this.
  - **Fix:** Add an Android VirtualReceiptTile equivalent (QR of the public receipt URL + open/copy buttons) shown when e.receiptId != null, using receiptDetail.eReceiptEyebrow/eReceiptTitle/openInBrowser/copyLink/scanHint/linkCopied keys.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] Currency converter tile missing on detail**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:74,372-395` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS shows a CurrencyConverterCard (PLN/EUR/USD glance of the expense amount, source badge, as-of/static-fallback labels). Android detail has no converter.
  - **Fix:** Add a CurrencyConverterCard equivalent on the detail screen using the expense currency as source and PLN/EUR/USD targets (converter.eyebrow/title/asOfFmt/staticFallback/source keys exist).
- 🟡 **[bug] Receipt item price shows '0,00' when price/totalPrice absent instead of hiding it**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:334-338` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:96-100`
  - iOS only renders the price Text when item.displayPrice (price ?? totalPrice) is non-nil. Android computes (item.price ?: item.totalPrice)?.toDouble() ?: 0.0 and always renders it, so items with no price show a misleading '0,00' formatted amount.
  - **Fix:** Only render the price Text when (item.price ?: item.totalPrice) is non-null; omit it otherwise to match iOS.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Detail screen reloads full expenses list per open; no shared warm store, no detail loading parity**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:30-46,48-57` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:41-55,65-68`
  - iOS reads the expense from the shared AppDataStore (instant when warm), shows NBSkeletonHero + NBSkeletonList(2) while loading, and a not-found NBErrorCard with retry. Android calls ExpensesRepo.list() on every detail open (extra network round-trip + latency), shows a generic NBLoadingCard, and on missing/error uses NBErrorCard whose retry actually calls onBack (navigates away) rather than retrying.
  - **Fix:** Introduce a shared store/state (or pass the expense in) so detail opens instantly; show skeleton hero+rows while loading; and make the not-found NBErrorCard retry re-run the load (onRetry = reload), not onBack. Use expenseDetail.notFound for the message.
- 🟡 **[copy-l10n] Hardcoded English fallback strings ('Failed', 'Not found') bypass existing L10n keys**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:55,128-156` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:66; android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:53,68`
  - Android uses literal English 'Failed' (ExpensesViewModel.load catch, ExpenseDetailScreen LaunchedEffect onFailure) and 'Not found' (NBErrorCard message fallback). iOS uses localized error copy and expenseDetail.notFound. These literals are never translated to Polish.
  - **Fix:** Replace 'Failed' with a localized generic error (e.g. locale.t("toast.error")) and 'Not found' with locale.t("expenseDetail.notFound"); both keys exist in core/L10n.kt.
- 🟡 **[visual] Detail action is a 'Close' button instead of iOS Edit/Delete actions card**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:397-417` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:107`
  - iOS shows an actions card with a secondary Edit button and a destructive Delete button (back navigation is the nav bar). Android instead shows a single NBSecondaryButton labeled common.close that just calls onBack, which is not present on iOS and replaces the actual actions.
  - **Fix:** Replace the Close button with an actions card containing a secondary Edit button and a destructive Delete button (common.edit / common.delete), relying on the system/back arrow for navigation as on iOS.
- ⚪ **[missing-feature] Receipt items card lacks count badge and loading/empty states**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:275-316` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:84-105`
  - iOS items card shows a count badge next to the 'Items' eyebrow, a loading spinner+'Loading' state, and a 'No items' state when the receipt has none. Android only renders the items card when receiptItems is non-empty (it is hidden entirely if the receipt has no items, and there is no loading/empty/count UI), and never shows it for receipts that loaded zero items.
  - **Fix:** Always render the items card when e.receiptId != null, add the count badge next to the Items eyebrow, a loading row, and a 'No items' (expenseDetail.noItems) empty state mirroring iOS.
- ⚪ **[missing-feature] Pull-to-refresh missing on both list and detail**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:86-91; native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:88-94` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS supports pull-to-refresh on both the list (force dashboard refresh + haptics) and the detail (refresh + reload receipt items). Android has neither.
  - **Fix:** Wrap the list and detail in a PullToRefresh container that re-invokes load()/reload and reloads receipt items, matching iOS.
- ⚪ **[behavior] No haptics on any expense interaction**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpensesListView.swift:267,559,681; native-ios/Solvio/Features/Expenses/ExpenseDetailView.swift:101,139` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpensesListScreen.kt:97-143; android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/expenses/ExpenseDetailScreen.kt:30-110`
  - iOS fires Haptics on selection, filter/sort toggles, row taps, delete warnings, and success/error toasts throughout. Android performs none of these haptic cues.
  - **Fix:** Add equivalent HapticFeedback calls (selection on toggles/taps, warning/error/success on delete/mutation outcomes) to match iOS feedback parity.

## Root/Navigation/Shell — parity ~52% (24 findings)

_The Android root shell reproduces the broad structure (sticky 56dp header, 5-slot bottom bar, floating "+" FAB, hamburger drawer) and reuses the same design tokens (Spacing/Radius/Shadow/Border all numerically identical), so the skeleton is close. But there are severe functional gaps: the FAB is completely dead — tapping it sets router.showingScanSheet but NOTHING observes that flag, so the ScanFabSheet is never presented (it is defined but orphaned). The ScanFabSheet itself is wired with the wrong signature/options (3 options with parallel L10n keys vs iOS's 4 options including Quick Split, and it uses a different copy set). The MoreSheet shows a full 10-item legacy menu while iOS deliberately slimmed its drawer to Settings + Sign Out only. There is no ToastOverlay, no ScanQueueWidget mounted in the shell, no post-scan savings-insight toast deep-link, no WelcomeOnboarding gate, no haptics anywhere, no active-tab top indicator bar, a degraded splash (spinner vs branded logo/tagline), a fragile 450ms boot delay instead of a real isRestoring flag, a wrong header bottom-border (1dp box on all sides vs iOS 2dp foreground bottom-only), and a wrong brand wordmark font (bold(18) vs iOS black(14)). Per-tab back stacks for Deals/Groups/Savings are missing entirely. Parity is moderate visually but low behaviorally._

- 🔴 **[bug] FAB is dead — ScanFabSheet is never presented**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:48-51,100-104` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:158; ScanFabSheet.kt:46`
  - iOS taps FAB -> router.showingScanSheet=true -> .sheet(isPresented:) presents ScanFabSheet. On Android, FloatingScanFab sets router.showingScanSheet.value=true but NOTHING observes that state; MainTabScreen never instantiates ScanFabSheet (grep shows ScanFabSheet is only referenced in its own file). The entire add-receipt entry point is non-functional.
  - **Fix:** In MainTabScreen, observe router.showingScanSheet and render the ScanFabSheet composable when true (e.g. if (router.showingScanSheet.value) ScanFabSheet(onDismiss = { router.showingScanSheet.value = false }, ...)). Wire its callbacks to camera, virtual-receipt, photo-library, and quick-split flows.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] ScanFabSheet has 3 options; iOS has 4 (Quick Split missing)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:575-608` → Android: `android/.../features/root/ScanFabSheet.kt:89-109`
  - iOS ScanFabSheet offers four options in order: Take Photo (camera, primary), Photo Library, Virtual receipt, Quick Split (person.2.fill). Android offers only three: Scan receipt (primary), Virtual receipt, Pick from library — Quick Split is absent AND order differs (virtual before library). iOS order is camera/library/virtual/quickSplit.
  - **Fix:** Add a fourth ActionTile for Quick Split (icon person.2 equivalent, key quickSplit.title + scanFab.quickSplitSub) and reorder to camera, library, virtual, quickSplit to match iOS. Wire quickSplit to a QuickSplitStandaloneSheet flow.
- 🟠 **[copy-l10n] ScanFabSheet uses divergent copy / wrong L10n keys**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:568-607; Core/L10n.swift:558-563,637,653-654,545` → Android: `android/.../features/root/ScanFabSheet.kt:76,91-108; core/L10n.kt:332-338`
  - iOS uses scanFab.title='Nowy paragon', scanFab.subtitle, and option titles from receipts.takePhoto/receipts.photoLibrary/receipts.virtual/quickSplit.title with subs scanFab.cameraSub/librarySub/virtualSub/quickSplitSub. Android invented parallel keys scanFab.scanReceipt/virtualReceipt/pickFromLibrary (+Sub) and scanFab.title='Dodaj paragon' (different wording) with abbreviated subs ('Aparat -> OCR' vs 'Szybki skan paragonu przez kamerę'). Also iOS shows a title+subtitle as page header (pageTitle + caption); Android shows only an NBEyebrow + a close X.
  - **Fix:** Replace Android keys with the iOS canonical ones (scanFab.title, scanFab.subtitle, receipts.takePhoto/photoLibrary/virtual, quickSplit.title, scanFab.cameraSub/librarySub/virtualSub/quickSplitSub). Render scanFab.title as pageTitle and scanFab.subtitle as caption below it (no eyebrow), matching iOS layout.
- 🟠 **[behavior] MoreSheet shows full legacy 10-item menu; iOS shows only Settings**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:484-488,500-528` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MoreSheet.kt:72-83,114-125`
  - iOS deliberately slimmed the drawer: items = [(.settings,...)] ONLY, plus a Sign Out destructive button. The comment explicitly says everything else moved to the Deals/Savings tabs. Android lists 10 rows (receipts, goals, challenges, loyalty, prices, audit, analysis, reports, categories, settings) + sign out. This is a different IA and shows entries iOS intentionally removed.
  - **Fix:** Reduce Android MoreSheet items to just Settings + Sign Out to match iOS. If those secondary screens must remain reachable on Android, surface them from the Deals/Savings tabs as iOS does, not from the drawer.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] No ToastOverlay mounted in the shell**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/RootView.swift:24` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS RootView always overlays ToastOverlay() above the whole app so toasts (success/error/actionable) render on any screen. Android RootScreen/MainActivity never mount any toast UI (grep for ToastOverlay returns nothing), even though a ToastCenter singleton is provided via LocalToast. Any code calling toast.success/error will have no visible effect.
  - **Fix:** Add a ToastOverlay composable observing LocalToast and overlay it at the top of RootScreen (or AppRoot's Surface) so toasts show globally, mirroring iOS.
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] ScanQueueWidget not mounted in the shell**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:58-59` → Android: `absent (MainTabScreen.kt has no ScanQueueWidget)`
  - iOS stacks ScanQueueWidget() above the tab bar (and below the FAB) so background receipt uploads show progress on every tab. Android has a ScanQueueWidget composable in core/ui but MainTabScreen never renders it (grep confirms zero references in MainTabScreen.kt). Background scan progress is invisible.
  - **Fix:** In MainTabScreen's bottom-end Column (above NBTabBar), render ScanQueueWidget() above/below the FAB to match iOS stacking, with the same padding (FAB then widget then bar).
- 🟡 **[visual] MoreSheet presentation: bottom sheet vs full NavigationStack drawer with title/close**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:490-551` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MoreSheet.kt:85-141`
  - iOS MoreSheet is a NavigationStack sheet with navigationTitle nav.more ('Więcej'), inline title display, and a Close toolbar button (common.close). It puts the user email at top as caption, items as nbCard rows (radius sm, shadow sm), and the sign-out as NBDestructiveButtonStyle button. Android renders a ModalBottomSheet with an NBEyebrow of nav.settings ('USTAWIENIA') as the header (wrong title — should be 'Więcej'/More), no navigation title, no Close button, and sign-out as just another bordered row rather than a primary destructive button.
  - **Fix:** Header text should use nav.more, not nav.settings. Add a Close affordance. Consider matching the iOS row card style (nbCard radius sm). Render sign-out using the NB destructive button atom rather than a plain MoreRow.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Post-scan savings-insight actionable toast + deep-link missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:69-81` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS observes scanQueue.savingsInsight and raises an actionable toast ('Zobacz') that switches to the Deals tab and sets router.pendingAnalyzeReceiptId to auto-run analysis. Android MainTabScreen has no equivalent onChange/collect of any savingsInsight, no pendingAnalyzeReceiptId field on AppRouter, and no deep-link into the Deals analysis.
  - **Fix:** Add pendingAnalyzeReceiptId to AppRouter, observe ScanQueueManager savingsInsight in MainTabScreen, raise an actionable toast that sets selectedTab=Deals and pendingAnalyzeReceiptId, and have OkazjeHubScreen consume it.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] WelcomeOnboarding gate missing from root switchboard**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/RootView.swift:8,17-19` → Android: `android/.../features/root/RootScreen.kt:37-42`
  - iOS RootView shows WelcomeOnboardingView (once per install, gated by @AppStorage solvio.seenWelcome) between splash and login. Android RootScreen has only three states (splash/main/login) with no welcome/onboarding carousel and no seenWelcome persistence (grep finds none).
  - **Fix:** Add a seenWelcome flag (DataStore/SharedPreferences) and a WelcomeOnboarding composable; in RootScreen show it when user==null and !seenWelcome, before LoginScreen.
- 🟡 **[visual] Splash is a bare spinner vs branded logo + wordmark + tagline**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/RootView.swift:31-52` → Android: `android/.../features/root/RootScreen.kt:44-55`
  - iOS SplashView shows the SplashLogo image (96x96, radius 22, 2px border, lg shadow), the brand wordmark in black(32), and an NBEyebrow tagline (splash.tagline). Android SplashScreen shows only a Material3 CircularProgressIndicator on the background — completely off-brand and not neobrutalist.
  - **Fix:** Rebuild SplashScreen to show the logo image (rounded 22dp, 2dp border, lg shadow), login.brand in black(32), and NBEyebrow(splash.tagline), matching iOS.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Boot uses fragile 450ms delay instead of an isRestoring flag**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/RootView.swift:13-15; Core/Session/SessionStore.swift:25,60` → Android: `android/.../features/root/RootScreen.kt:27-41`
  - iOS gates splash on session.isRestoring (set true at init, false in a defer after restore completes) — deterministic. Android SessionStore has no isRestoring; RootScreen hardcodes delay(450) then bootDone=true, so a slow network leaks login flash, and a fast restore wastes time on splash. Also if user==null after a real restore, splash can flash login during the window.
  - **Fix:** Add isRestoring: StateFlow<Boolean> to SessionStore (true initially, false after restore() finishes). In RootScreen show SplashScreen while isRestoring, then user!=null -> MainTabScreen else welcome/login. Remove the delay hack.
- 🟡 **[behavior] No haptic feedback anywhere in the shell**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:49,435,438,583-604` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS fires Haptics.impact(.medium) on FAB tap, Haptics.impact(.light) on tap-to-pop-to-root, Haptics.selection() on tab swap and on every scan-option tap. Android has zero haptics (grep for Haptic/performHapticFeedback across the app returns nothing).
  - **Fix:** Use LocalHapticFeedback / View.performHapticFeedback on FAB tap, tab change, tap-to-pop, and scan-option taps to mirror iOS feedback semantics.
- 🟡 **[visual] Active tab has no top indicator bar**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:453-460` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:239-256`
  - iOS draws a 3pt foreground indicator bar at the top of the active tab slot (offset y -2) and adjusts top padding (6 active vs 8 inactive). Android TabSlot has no top indicator rectangle and no active/inactive top-padding difference — the only active cue is color + (intended) font weight.
  - **Fix:** Add a 3dp foreground Box at the top edge of the active TabSlot (aligned top, slight negative offset) and vary top padding 6dp/8dp by active state, matching iOS.
- 🟡 **[visual] Header bottom divider: full 1dp box border vs iOS 2dp foreground bottom-only**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:312-318` → Android: `android/.../features/root/AppMobileHeader.kt:59`
  - iOS header has background opacity 0.95 and a single bottom divider Rectangle filled with Theme.foreground at Border.width (2dp). Android applies .border(SolvioTheme.Border.widthThin, palette.border) which draws a 1dp box on ALL four sides using border color (not foreground) and uses fully opaque background. Visually wrong divider weight, color, and placement.
  - **Fix:** Replace the full border with a bottom-only 2dp foreground divider (e.g. drawBehind a line at the bottom edge, color=palette.foreground, height=Border.width). Optionally apply 0.95 background alpha to match.
- 🟡 **[visual] Brand wordmark font: bold(18) vs iOS black(14) with negative tracking**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:294-297` → Android: `android/.../features/root/AppMobileHeader.kt:95-98`
  - iOS brand wordmark is AppFont.black(14) with tracking -0.5. Android uses SolvioFonts.bold(18) with no letter spacing — heavier-by-size yet lighter weight and wider tracking, noticeably larger and visually different.
  - **Fix:** Use SolvioFonts.black(14) with letterSpacing approx (-0.5).sp for the brand text to match iOS.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Per-tab navigation back stacks missing for Deals/Groups/Savings**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:203-217; Core/AppRouter.swift:64-68` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:147-149`
  - iOS wraps every tab (dashboard, expenses, deals, groups, savings) in its own NavigationStack with routeDestinations, so each tab keeps an independent push stack (e.g. groupDetail, groupReceipts, groupSettlements, goalDetail). Android gives Dashboard and Expenses a NavHost but renders Deals/Groups/Savings as bare screens (OkazjeHubScreen/GroupsListScreen/SavingsHubScreen) with no NavHost — so e.g. tapping a group cannot push GroupDetail within the Groups tab.
  - **Fix:** Wrap Deals, Groups, and Savings in their own rememberNavController()/NavHost with the corresponding sub-destinations (groupDetail/groupReceipts/groupSettlements, goalDetail, etc.), mirroring iOS per-tab stacks.
- 🟡 **[behavior] Tap active tab to pop-to-root not implemented**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:431-440` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:163,242`
  - iOS: tapping the already-selected tab calls router.popToRoot() (with light haptic); tapping a different tab switches with a selection haptic. Android TabSlot's onSelect always just sets selectedTab.value=it; tapping the current tab does nothing (no pop-to-root) and there's no per-tab popBackStack call.
  - **Fix:** In NBTabBar onSelect, if the tapped tab == current selected, pop that tab's NavController to its start destination; otherwise switch tabs. Add a popToRoot helper on AppRouter or per-tab nav controllers.
- 🟡 **[behavior] MoreSheet routing collapses everything onto the Dashboard tab; ignores iOS tabForMoreRoute split**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:82-95; Core/AppRouter.swift:117-124` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:170-182,95-136`
  - iOS routes MoreRoutes to specific tabs via tabForMoreRoute (prices/audit/shoppingAdvisor/nearbyStores/productSearch -> Deals tab; others -> Savings tab) and pushes onto that tab's stack. Android instead hosts ALL secondary routes on the Dashboard tab's NavHost and always switches to Dashboard then navigates, so audit/prices/etc. land on the wrong tab context. (Also iOS's actual slim drawer makes most of this moot, but if Android keeps the items the tab mapping is wrong.)
  - **Fix:** Either slim the drawer to Settings only (preferred, matches iOS), or implement tabForMoreRoute-equivalent mapping so prices/audit/etc. open under the Deals tab and others under Savings, pushing onto the correct tab's NavHost.
- ⚪ **[visual] Active tab label weight not changed (always mono regular)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:444-446` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:248-254`
  - iOS uses AppFont.monoBold(10) when active and AppFont.mono(10) when inactive, and the icon weight goes bold vs semibold. Android always uses SolvioFonts.mono(10) for the label regardless of active state (only color changes), and the Material icon has no weight variation.
  - **Fix:** Use SolvioFonts.monoBold(10) for the active tab label and mono(10) when inactive to match the iOS active-state typography emphasis.
- ⚪ **[visual] NBTabBar top border is a 2dp box on all sides vs iOS top-only divider**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:421-426` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:216`
  - iOS tab bar overlays a Theme.foreground rectangle of Border.width only at the TOP edge. Android uses .border(Border.width, palette.border) which draws a box on all four sides and uses border color instead of foreground. Bottom/side edges shouldn't have a stroke.
  - **Fix:** Replace the box .border with a top-only divider (drawBehind line at top, color=palette.foreground, thickness=Border.width).
- ⚪ **[visual] FAB stacking/padding does not reserve space for the ScanQueueWidget**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:41-61` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:155-159`
  - iOS stacks FAB, then ScanQueueWidget, then NBTabBar in a VStack so the widget naturally sits between FAB and bar without overlap, and content is padded bottom 64. Android positions only the FAB via align BottomEnd with bottom=76dp and never places the queue widget, so once the widget is added there is no defined stacking order/spacing and it will overlap the FAB or bar.
  - **Fix:** Replace the single-FAB Column with a bottom-aligned Column containing FAB -> ScanQueueWidget(padding 6dp) ordering above the tab bar, matching iOS VStack stacking, and verify content bottom padding reserves the bar height.
- ⚪ **[copy-l10n] scanFab.title copy differs (Dodaj paragon vs Nowy paragon) and splash.tagline differs**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Core/L10n.swift:558,1471; L10n+EN.swift:1433` → Android: `android/.../core/L10n.kt:332,62`
  - iOS scanFab.title PL='Nowy paragon'; Android='Dodaj paragon'. iOS splash.tagline PL='Inteligentne finanse' / EN='Smart finance'; Android PL='Mądre finanse' (EN matches 'Smart finance'). PL copy diverges in both.
  - **Fix:** Align Android L10n: scanFab.title -> 'Nowy paragon', splash.tagline PL -> 'Inteligentne finanse', so PL copy matches iOS exactly.
- ⚪ **[visual] Header icon tiles lack shadow consistency / left tile differs from iOS**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/Root/MainTabView.swift:269-279,342-359` → Android: `android/.../features/root/AppMobileHeader.kt:64-70,129-148`
  - iOS hamburger button is 36x36 (frame 36) on Theme.card with thin border; the right-side language/theme HeaderIconButtons are 32x32 on Theme.card. Android renders ALL three (hamburger + language + theme) via the same IconTile at 32x32 — so the hamburger is 32 vs iOS 36. Also iOS center wallet tile carries an nbShadow(sm); Android wallet tile does carry shadow (matches) but the plain IconTiles match iOS (no shadow), so only the hamburger size is off.
  - **Fix:** Make the hamburger IconTile 36x36 (icon 18sp/semibold) to match iOS, keeping the language/theme tiles at 32x32.
- ⚪ **[bug] TodoPlaceholder dead code references undefined visual contract**
  - iOS: `absent` → Android: `android/.../features/root/MainTabScreen.kt:191-204`
  - TodoPlaceholder is defined but unused (all composable bodies now call real screens). It is harmless dead code but signals the file was scaffolded; left in, it adds an unused private composable. Not a crash but should be removed for parity/cleanliness.
  - **Fix:** Delete the unused TodoPlaceholder composable.

## ProductSearch — parity ~42% (22 findings)

_Android's ProductSearchScreen.kt is a structurally different and significantly thinner port of iOS ProductSearchView.swift. The data models match perfectly, but the UI diverges heavily. Android invents a "HeroCard" layout (product name + large hero price + average + a fabricated RANGE column) that does not exist on iOS, while it omits iOS's two side-by-side stat pills (cheapest + average with icons). Android is missing the entire Recent Searches history feature (persisted chips + clear button), does not sort results by price, drops the inline "cheapest" badge and green-price highlighting on the cheapest row, uses a plain spinner loading card instead of iOS's animated multi-stage NBProgressCard, hardcodes currency to "PLN" (iOS reads a sticky EUR/USD-aware currency), and formats prices with a localized currency formatter that produces different strings than iOS's "%.2f CUR" format. It also uses a different L10n key namespace (productSearch.* vs iOS search.*) with several missing keys and a hardcoded "RANGE" string, has a different search-bar structure (no clear button, no submit-on-keyboard, button below instead of beside), and omits the navigation title. Roughly half of the screen matches at the atom level; the overall layout and several features do not._

- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Recent Searches history feature entirely missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:21-156` → Android: `absent`
  - iOS persists the last 8 unique queries in UserDefaults (key 'solvio.productSearch.history'), surfaces them as tappable clock-icon chips when query is empty and there is no result, with a 'RECENT SEARCHES' label and a 'Clear' button (search.recent / search.clearHistory). Tapping a chip re-runs the search; successful searches are saved move-to-front, deduped case-insensitively, capped at 8. Android has none of this — no history state, no persistence, no chips, no clear button.
  - **Fix:** Add a persisted history list to ProductSearchViewModel (SharedPreferences key 'solvio.productSearch.history', limit 8, move-to-front dedup). Render a RecentSearches composable (horizontal scrollable chips with Icons.Default.History, muted background, thin border, Radius.sm) gated on query.isBlank() && state is Idle && history not empty. Add a 'Clear' text button. Add L10n keys productSearch.recent and productSearch.clearHistory (or reuse search.recent/search.clearHistory which already exist).
- 🟠 **[bug] Currency hardcoded to PLN instead of sticky user currency**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:15,84` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:74`
  - iOS uses `store.currency` (AppDataStore, prefetched from dashboard, falls back to UserDefaults) so a user who set EUR/USD never sees PLN, and passes that currency to the search request and to all price formatting. Android hardcodes `val currency = "PLN"` both for the network request and all formatting. AnalysisScreen on Android already fetches currency via SettingsRepo.fetch().settings.currency, so the pattern exists but is not used here.
  - **Fix:** Fetch the user's currency in ProductSearchViewModel via SettingsRepo.fetch().settings.currency (cache it, default PLN), expose as StateFlow, and use it both in vm.search(...) and in every Fmt.amount(...) call. Do not hardcode "PLN".
- 🟠 **[behavior] Price string format differs from iOS (localized currency vs plain code)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:418-420` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:167,177,184,210,244`
  - iOS formatPrice uses String(format: "%.2f %@", value, currency) producing e.g. '12.50 PLN'. Android Fmt.amount uses NumberFormat.getCurrencyInstance(Locale.getDefault()) producing a localized symbol/placement, e.g. '12,50 zł' or '$12.50'. The two apps print different strings for the same data, breaking parity, and Android's decimal separator and symbol position vary with device locale.
  - **Fix:** For this screen mirror iOS exactly: format as '%.2f <CURRENCY_CODE>' (e.g. add Fmt.amountCode(value, currency) -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f %s", value, currency)) and use it everywhere this screen formats a price. Avoid NumberFormat.getCurrencyInstance here.
- 🟠 **[visual] Summary stat pills replaced by a fabricated HeroCard layout**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:223-262` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:124,142-191`
  - iOS shows a summaryBar of up to two equal-width statPills side by side: 'Cheapest' (arrow.down.circle.fill icon, success color, value=cheapestPrice) and 'Average' (equal.circle.fill icon, info color, value=averagePrice), each an nbCard(Radius.sm, Shadow.sm) with bold(16) value + mono(10) uppercase label. Android instead renders a single HeroCard (Radius.lg, Shadow.lg) containing the product name, a hero(36) success-colored cheapest price, the cheapest store name, plus Average and RANGE columns. The whole structure, sizing, icons, and shadows differ.
  - **Fix:** Replace HeroCard with a Row of two NBStatTile/stat-pill cards matching iOS: cheapest (down-arrow icon, success) and average (equals icon, info), Radius.sm/Shadow.sm, value style bold(16), label mono(10) uppercase muted, frame weight 1f each, spacing Theme.Spacing.md. Drop the hero price, the product/store name, and the RANGE column.
- 🟠 **[behavior] Results not sorted by price ascending**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:269-270` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:126`
  - iOS sorts results ascending by price (nil prices sink to the bottom via .infinity) before rendering. Android renders r.results in raw API order with no sort, so the cheapest is not necessarily first and ordering can differ from iOS.
  - **Fix:** Sort before rendering: items(r.results.sortedBy { it.price ?: Double.MAX_VALUE }) (build the sorted list once, then index into it).
- 🟠 **[missing-feature] Inline 'Cheapest' badge + green price on cheapest result row missing**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:271,285-326` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:126,194-228`
  - iOS computes isCheapest = item.store == cheapestStore for each row, shows a green 'Najtaniej/Cheapest' pill (mono(9), success background, radius 4) next to the store name, and renders that row's price in success color with bold(18). Android's ResultRow has no isCheapest parameter, no cheapest badge, and always renders the price in foreground color with monoBold(14).
  - **Fix:** Pass isCheapest (store == r.cheapestStore) into ResultRow; render an NBTag/pill with success background + productSearch.cheapest label when true; color the price palette.success when isCheapest, else foreground; use a bold ~18sp price style to match iOS bold(18).
- 🟠 **[behavior] Animated multi-stage NBProgressCard replaced by plain NBLoadingCard**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:36-47` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:108`
  - iOS loading state uses NBProgressCard with title 'productSearch.runningTitle' and 4 rotating localized stages (progress.preparingRequest, progress.scanningWeb, progress.matchingProducts, progress.almostDone), a 95%-capped animated progress bar and elapsed/ETA counter, estimatedSeconds:10. Android shows the generic NBLoadingCard (a spinner + 'common.loading'), losing the staged messaging, bar, and ETA. The Android NBProgressCard composable already exists but is unused here.
  - **Fix:** Replace NBLoadingCard() with NBProgressCard(title = locale.t("productSearch.runningTitle"), stages = listOf(locale.t("progress.preparingRequest"), locale.t("progress.scanningWeb"), locale.t("progress.matchingProducts"), locale.t("progress.almostDone")), estimatedSeconds = 10).
- 🟠 **[visual] Search bar structure differs (no clear button, no keyboard submit, button below not beside)**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:170-219` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:88-104`
  - iOS search bar is a single HStack: a card-styled text field (magnifyingglass leading icon, placeholder, and an xmark.circle.fill clear button that also clears the result when query non-empty) plus a 44x44 foreground-colored square arrow.right submit button beside it; submitting via the keyboard 'search' key also triggers search. Android wraps an NBTextField inside an NBCard with a full-width NBPrimaryButton stacked below; there is no leading magnifier icon, no clear (x) button, no result clearing, and no keyboard 'search' submit action.
  - **Fix:** Rebuild the search bar as a Row: NBTextField with a leading search icon and a trailing clear icon (clears query and resets state to Idle when query non-empty), plus a 44dp square submit button (foreground background, arrow icon) beside it; wire NBTextField imeAction=Search / onSearch to run the query.
- 🟡 **[missing-feature] priceRange (RANGE) displayed on Android but never shown on iOS**
  - iOS: `native-ios/Solvio/Features/ProductSearch/ProductSearchView.swift:223-242` → Android: `android/app/src/main/java/com/programo/solvio/features/productsearch/ProductSearchScreen.kt:180-188`
  - iOS never reads or displays response.priceRange anywhere in the view. Android invents a RANGE column in the HeroCard rendering '<min> – <max>'. This is content that does not exist on iOS.
